Extract text from all pages\n    all_text = \"\"\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        all_text += page.extract_text() + \"\\n\"\n\n    print(all_text)\n```\n\n#### Extract from Specific Page\n```python\nwith pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:\n    first_page_text = pdf.pages[0].extract_text()\n    print(first_page_text)\n```\n\n#### Search for Keywords\n```python\nimport re\n\nwith pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:\n    all_text = \"\"\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        all_text += page.extract_text() + \"\\n\"\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_0141TX83WBDEBrdxHrHNzAyc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01NajpojQP652tRUjJ8Ete5n","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/environment/skills/temperature-profile-analysis.md","content":"# Temperature Profile Analysis for Reflow Soldering\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers analyzing thermal profiles from reflow soldering processes, including calculating ramp rates, time above liquidus, and peak temperature compliance.\n\n## Key Concepts from Manufacturing\n\n### Reflow Profile Stages\n1. **Preheat**: Gradually heat the assembly (100-150°C)\n2. **Dryout**: Evaporate solder paste volatiles\n3. **Reflow**: Peak temperature zone for solder melting\n4. **Cooling**: Cool assembly after reflow\n\n### Ramp Rate Calculation\nRamp rate is the temperature change per unit time:\n\n```\nRamp Rate (°C/s) = ΔTemperature / ΔTime\n                 = (T2 - T1) / (t2 - t1)\n```\n\nFor preheat zone analysis:\n1. Identify data points within preheat temperature range\n2. Calculate rate between consecutive time points\n3. Find maximum rate\n4. Compare against specification limit\n\n### Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\nTAL measures how long the solder remains above its melting point (liquidus temperature).\n\n```\nTAL (seconds) = Total time when temperature ≥ liquidus temperature\n```\n\nSteps:\n1. Get liquidus temperature from solder type\n2. Filter data points ≥ liquidus temperature\n3. Calculate time span from first to last point above liquidus\n4. Or count duration by time intervals\n\n### Peak Temperature\nMaximum temperature reached during reflow zone.\n\n```python\npeak_temp = df[df['run_id'] == run_id]['temp_c'].max()\n```\n\n## Implementation Patterns\n\n### Extract Preheat Zone Data\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Thermocouple data has time_s and temp_c columns\n# Preheat zone: typically 100°C to 150°C\npreheat_data = df[(df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                  (df['temp_c'] >= 100) &\n                  (df['temp_c'] <= 150)]\n\n# Sort by time to ensure proper sequencing\npreheat_data = preheat_data.sort_values('time_s')\n```\n\n### Calculate Ramp Rate\n```python\ndef calculate_ramp_rate(temps, times):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate ramp rate as rate of temperature change.\n    temps: list of temperature values (Celsius)\n    times: list of time values (seconds)\n\n    Returns: maximum ramp rate in °C/s\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(temps) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:  # Avoid division by zero\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    return max(ramp_rates) if ramp_rates else None\n```\n\n### Calculate Time Above Liquidus\n```python\ndef calculate_tal(df_run, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL).\n\n    df_run: DataFrame with time_s and temp_c for one run/thermocouple\n    liquidus_temp: Solder liquidus temperature in Celsius\n\n    Returns: TAL in seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter points above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = df_run[df_run['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    time_values = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = time_values[-1] - time_values[0]\n\n    return tal\n```\n\n### Select Representative Thermocouple\nAccording to IPC standards, use the thermocouple at the location of greatest thermal mass:\n- Typically labeled \"largest_mass\" or similar in industrial data\n- Provides most conservative (worst-case) profile\n\n```python\n# Priority order for selection\ntc_priority = ['largest_mass', 'max_mass', 'board_center', 'TC1']\n\ndef get_representative_tc(df_run, tc_options):\n    \"\"\"Select TC based on priority\"\"\"\n    for tc_option in tc_priority:\n        matching = df_run[df_run['tc_location'] == tc_option]\n        if not matching.empty:\n            return tc_option\n    # Fallback to first available\n    return df_run['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n```\n\n## Common Specifications Reference\n\n### Standard Limits (typical values)\n- **Preheat ramp rate**: < 2°C/s (per IPC-A-610)\n- **Reflow peak temperature**: 245-260°C (for SAC305/lead-free)\n- **TAL (Time Above Liquidus)**: 30-90 seconds typical range\n- **Conveyor speed**: Depends on zone dwell requirements\n\n## Validation Patterns\n\n### Check Data Continuity\n```python\n# Ensure time is monotonically increasing\nassert df['time_s'].is_monotonic_increasing, \"Time not monotonic\"\n\n# Check for reasonable temperature transitions\ntemp_diffs = df['temp_c'].diff()\nassert (temp_diffs < 50).all(), \"Unrealistic temperature change\"\n```\n\n### Handle Missing Data\n```python\n# Check for null values\nif df[['temp_c', 'time_s']].isnull().any().any():\n    print(\"Warning: Missing data detected\")\n    df = df.dropna(subset=['temp_c', 'time_s'])\n```\n\n### Verify Run Completeness\n```python\n# Check if run has thermocouple data\nif df[df['run_id'] == run_id].empty:\n    print(f\"No thermocouple data for run {run_id}\")\n    # Mark as failing per requirements\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1392,"cache_read_input_tokens":36029,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1392,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"46d78ac3-9991-4b77-b821-fe30b16f9d57","uuid":"5f1830da-5fbc-46bf-b3ba-074b8526071f"}

Compare against specification limit\n\n### Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\nTAL measures how long the solder remains above its melting point (liquidus temperature).\n\n```\nTAL (seconds) = Total time when temperature ≥ liquidus temperature\n```\n\nSteps:\n1. times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:  # Avoid division by zero\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    return max(ramp_rates) if ramp_rates else None\n```\n\n### Calculate Time Above Liquidus\n```python\ndef calculate_tal(df_run, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL).\n\n    df_run: DataFrame with time_s and temp_c for one run/thermocouple\n    liquidus_temp: Solder liquidus temperature in Celsius\n\n    Returns: TAL in seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter points above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = df_run[df_run['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    time_values = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = time_values[-1] - Record continuous temperature profile\n2. Identify all data points ≥ liquidus temperature\n3. Calculate time span from first to last point above liquidus\n4. This is the TAL value\n\n## Conveyor Speed and Dwell Requirements\n\n### Process Speed Formula\n```\nLine Speed (cm/min) = (Board Length (cm) + Machine Length (cm)) / Dwell Time (min)\n```\n\nOr rearranged:\n```\nMinimum Conveyor Speed = Board Length / Thermal Dwell Time Required\n```\n\n### Dwell Time Calculation\nTotal required dwell time = sum of all zone dwell times\n- **Preheat dwell**: Time to reach 150°C\n- **Reflow dwell**: Time to complete reflow and cool\n- Varies by board size and thermal mass\n\n### Board Geometry Considerations\n- **Board Length**: Critical for conveyor speed calculation\n- **Board Width**: May affect thermal profile\n- **Thermal Mass**: Board weight, copper thickness, component density\n\nBoards with higher thermal mass need:\n- Longer dwell in preheat zone\n- Slower conveyor speeds (cm/min)\n\n### Conveyor Speed Constraints\n- **Minimum Speed**: Must achieve sufficient dwell time\n- **Maximum Speed**: Limited by machine design (typically 9-50 in/min)\n- **Edge Conveyor**: Uses thin rails (0.17\" contact), requires straightness\n\n## Peak Temperature Requirements\n\n### Minimum Peak Temperature\n- **Lead-free (SAC alloys)**: 245-250°C minimum\n- **Lead-based alloys**: 220-240°C minimum\n\nAdequate peak temperature ensures:\n- Complete solder melting\n- Good wetting and joint formation\n- Proper intermetallic layer development\n\n## Thermocouple Selection for Analysis\n\n### Representative Thermocouple Location\nUse the thermocouple at the location of **greatest thermal mass**:\n- Most thermally challenging location\n- Provides conservative (worst-case) measurement\n- Ensures full assembly reaches required temperatures\n\nIf multiple locations available, prioritize:\n1. \"largest_mass\" or \"max_mass\" location\n2. Board center with heavy components\n3. Largest component area\n\n## Process Compliance Verification\n\n### Compliance Checks\n1. **Preheat Ramp Rate**: Max value ≤ 2°C/s ✓\n2. **Peak Temperature**: Max value ≥ minimum required ✓\n3. **Time Above Liquidus**: Value within min/max range ✓\n4. **Conveyor Speed**: Actual ≥ minimum required ✓\n\nAll criteria must be met for production acceptance.\n\n## Common Defects and Root Causes\n\n### Defect: Slow Ramp Rate → Solder Spatter, Solder Balls\n- **Cause**: Insufficient preheat heating\n- **Result**: Excessive drying or incomplete drying\n- **Solution**: Increase preheat ramp rate (up to 2°C/s limit)\n\n### Defect: Excessive Ramp Rate → Component Cracking\n- **Cause**: Thermal shock on ceramic capacitors, BGA\n- **Result**: Micro-cracks in components\n- **Solution**: Reduce preheat ramp rate, increase board residence time\n\n### Defect: Low Peak Temperature → Insufficient Wetting, Cold Joints\n- **Cause**: Inadequate conveyor speed or zone setpoints too low\n- **Result**: Solder doesn't fully melt and reflow\n- **Solution**: Increase peak temperature setpoints or reduce conveyor speed\n\n### Defect: Excessive TAL → Brittle Joints, Intermetallic Growth\n- **Cause**: Slow conveyor speed, overheated oven\n- **Result**: Excessive intermetallic compounds form\n- **Solution**: Increase conveyor speed or reduce zone temperatures\n\n## Oven Types and Capabilities\n\n### Convection Reflow Oven\n- Uses heated air/nitrogen flow perpendicular to board\n- Better for uniform heating of complex assemblies\n- Slower heat transfer (lower efficiency)\n- Typical zone counts: 8-10 zones\n\n### IR (Infrared) Reflow Oven\n- Uses tungsten filament lamps as primary heat source\n- Faster response time (1-2 seconds)\n- Good for high thermal mass assemblies\n- Can have edge heating zones\n\n### Hybrid IR+Convection Oven\n- Combines benefits of both heating methods\n- Better uniformity and flexibility\n- Better temperature control\n\n## Loading Factor\n\n```\nLoading Factor = (Total Board Mass in Zone) / (Zone Heat Capacity)\n```\n\nHigher loading factor means:\n- More thermal mass to heat\n- Longer dwell times required\n- Slower conveyor speeds needed\n\nTypical range: 0.55-1.25 (higher = more challenging)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1547,"cache_read_input_tokens":37421,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1547,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"46d78ac3-9991-4b77-b821-fe30b16f9d57","uuid":"0154449f-4505-47eb-abc9-8b9b2d80bc87"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_0157oqF3f4avqe4fS4EbSNbB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01MrEEn9NcZNRdTk5MaBbkex","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/environment/skills/quality-and-efficiency-metrics.md","content":"# Manufacturing Quality and Efficiency Metrics\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers calculating and comparing manufacturing quality and efficiency metrics for process optimization and run selection.\n\n## Quality Metrics\n\n### Yield Percentage\n```\nYield % = (Units Passed / Total Units Tested) × 100\n```\n\n- Higher yield is better\n- Directly impacts cost and reliability\n- Target: >95% typically for SMT production\n\n### First Pass Yield (FPY)\nFrom test defect data:\n- `fp_yield_pct`: Percentage of units passing on first attempt\n- Includes rework and scrap in calculation\n- Better than simple yield as it accounts for defects caught\n\n### Defect Rate\n```\nDefect Rate = Number of Defects / Total Units Tested\n```\n\n- Lower is better\n- Can be analyzed by severity (critical, major, minor)\n- Can be analyzed by location (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4)\n\n### Critical Defect Count\nDefects with severity = 'critical' indicate serious issues:\n- Insufficient solder (incomplete melting)\n- Bridging (shorts between traces)\n- Component cracks (thermal shock damage)\n\nFocus on eliminating critical defects first.\n\n## Efficiency Metrics\n\n### Conveyor Speed (cm/min)\n- How fast the board travels through reflow oven\n- Higher speed = higher throughput\n- Must meet minimum for thermal compliance\n\n### Boards Per Minute (BPM)\n```\nBPM = Conveyor Speed × Board Count / Board Length\n```\n\nFrom MES log: `boards_per_min` field\n\n- Direct measure of equipment output\n- Higher is better for productivity\n- Must balance with quality\n\n### Dwell Time in Oven\n```\nDwell Time (s) = Total Zone Length / Conveyor Speed (cm/s)\n```\n\n- Time board spends in reflow oven\n- Must be sufficient for thermal profile requirements\n- Longer dwell = slower conveyor = lower throughput\n\n### Downtime\nFrom MES log: `downtime_s` field\n- Machine downtime in seconds per run\n- Equipment failures, adjustments, maintenance\n- Lower is better for efficiency\n- 0 downtime = uninterrupted operation\n\n## Composite Quality-Efficiency Score\n\nTo select best run per board family, create a scoring system:\n\n```python\ndef calculate_run_score(run_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Composite score combining quality and efficiency.\n    Higher score = better run.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Quality component (0-50 points)\n    quality_score = run_data['fp_yield_pct'] / 2  # 0-50 points\n\n    # Efficiency component (0-50 points)\n    # Normalize boards_per_min to 0-50 scale\n    max_bpm = 6  # Typical max BPM in dataset\n    efficiency_score = min(50, (run_data['boards_per_min'] / max_bpm) * 50)\n\n    # Downtime penalty (deduct from efficiency)\n    downtime_penalty = min(10, run_data['downtime_s'] / 100)  # Penalty up to 10 points\n\n    total_score = quality_score + (efficiency_score - downtime_penalty)\n\n    return total_score\n```\n\n## Defect Analysis Patterns\n\n### Count Defects by Run\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Load test defects\ndefects_df = pd.read_csv('test_defects.csv')\n\n# Get all rows for a run (excluding summary rows)\nrun_defects = defects_df[(defects_df['run_id'] == 'R0001') &\n                         (defects_df['inspection_stage'] != 'SUMMARY')]\n\n# Critical defects\ncritical_count = len(run_defects[run_defects['severity'] == 'critical'])\n\n# Total defect count\ntotal_defects = run_defects['count'].sum()\n```\n\n### Summarize by Board Family\n```python\n# Merge with MES log to get board family\nmerged = pd.merge(defects_df, mes_log, on='run_id')\n\n# Group by board defects\n})\n```\n\n## Selection Criteria for Best Run\n\nRank runs per board family by:\n\n1. **First Pass Yield %** (primary criterion)\n   - Quality is paramount\n   - Higher yield = better process control\n   - Fewer rework costs\n\n2. **Boards Per Minute** (secondary criterion)\n   - Efficiency when quality is comparable\n   - Higher throughput = lower per-unit cost\n\n3. **Downtime** (tertiary criterion)\n   - Lower downtime = better reliability\n   - Indicates stable process\n\n4. **Critical Defect Count**\n   - Eliminate runs with critical defects immediately\n   - Even if yield% seems acceptable\n\n### Selection Algorithm\n```python\ndef select_best_run(board_family_runs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run for a board family.\n\n    board_family_runs: DataFrame with all runs for one family\n\n    Returns: best_run_id (string)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter out runs with critical defects\n    clean_runs = board_family_runs[board_family_runs['critical_defects'] == 0]\n\n    if clean_runs.empty:\n        # If all have critical defects, choose lowest defect count\n        clean_runs = board_family_runs\n\n    # Sort by yield (descending), then BPM (descending)\n    ranked = clean_runs.sort_values(\n        by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n        ascending=[False, False]\n    )\n\n    return ranked.iloc[0]['run_id'], ranked.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n```\n\n## Comparison Framework\n\n### Run Comparison Metrics\n```python\ndef compare_runs(run_ids, merged_data):\n    \"\"\"Create comparison table for runs\"\"\"\n    comparison = merged_data[merged_data['run_id'].isin(run_ids)].groupby('run_id').agg({\n        'fp_yield_pct': 'first',\n        'boards_per_min': 'first',\n        'downtime_s': 'first',\n /app/environment/skills/quality-and-efficiency-metrics.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"46d78ac3-9991-4b77-b821-fe30b16f9d57","uuid":"6202ab3c-02e2-4103-9fa8-a0f1291eb229","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/app/environment/skills/quality-and-efficiency-metrics.md","content":"# Manufacturing Quality and Efficiency Metrics\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers calculating and comparing manufacturing quality and efficiency metrics for process optimization and run selection.\n\n## Quality Metrics\n\n### Yield Percentage\n```\nYield % = (Units Passed / Total Units Tested) × 100\n```\n\n- Higher yield is better\n- Directly impacts cost and reliability\n- Target: >95% typically for SMT production\n\n### First Pass Yield (FPY)\nFrom test defect data:\n- `fp_yield_pct`: Percentage of units passing on first attempt\n- Includes rework and scrap in calculation\n- Better than simple yield as it accounts for defects caught\n\n### Defect Rate\n```\nDefect Rate = Number of Defects / Total Units Tested\n```\n\n- Lower is better\n- Can be analyzed by severity (critical, major, minor)\n- Can be analyzed by location (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4)\n\n### Critical Defect Count\nDefects with severity = 'critical' indicate serious issues:\n- Insufficient solder (incomplete melting)\n- Bridging (shorts between traces)\n- Component cracks (thermal shock damage)\n\nFocus on eliminating critical defects first.\n\n## Efficiency Metrics\n\n### Conveyor Speed (cm/min)\n- How fast the board travels through reflow oven\n- Higher speed = higher throughput\n- Must meet minimum for thermal compliance\n\n### Boards Per Minute (BPM)\n```\nBPM = Conveyor Speed × Board Count / Board Length\n```\n\nFrom MES log: `boards_per_min` field\n\n- Direct measure of equipment output\n- Higher is better for productivity\n- Must balance with quality\n\n### Dwell Time in Oven\n```\nDwell Time (s) = Total Zone Length / Conveyor Speed (cm/s)\n```\n\n- Time board spends in reflow oven\n- Must be sufficient for thermal profile requirements\n- Longer dwell = slower conveyor = lower throughput\n\n### Downtime\nFrom MES log: `downtime_s` field\n- Machine downtime in seconds per run\n- Equipment failures, adjustments, maintenance\n- Lower is better for efficiency\n- 0 downtime = uninterrupted operation\n\n## Composite Quality-Efficiency Score\n\nTo select best run per board family, create a scoring system:\n\n```python\ndef calculate_run_score(run_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Composite score combining quality and efficiency.\n    Higher score = better run.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Quality component (0-50 points)\n    quality_score = run_data['fp_yield_pct'] / 2  # 0-50 points\n\n    # Efficiency component (0-50 points)\n    # Normalize boards_per_min to 0-50 scale\n    max_bpm = 6  # Typical max BPM in dataset\n    efficiency_score = min(50, (run_data['boards_per_min'] / max_bpm) * 50)\n\n    # Downtime penalty (deduct from efficiency)\n    downtime_penalty = min(10, run_data['downtime_s'] / 100)  # Penalty up to 10 points\n\n    total_score = quality_score + (efficiency_score - downtime_penalty)\n\n    return total_score\n```\n\n## Defect Analysis Patterns\n\n### Count Defects by Run\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Load test defects\ndefects_df = pd.read_csv('test_defects.csv')\n\n# Get all rows for a run (excluding summary rows)\nrun_defects = defects_df[(defects_df['run_id'] == 'R0001') &\n                         (defects_df['inspection_stage'] != 'SUMMARY')]\n\n# Critical defects\ncritical_count = len(run_defects[run_defects['severity'] == 'critical'])\n\n# Total defect count\ntotal_defects = run_defects['count'].sum()\n```\n\n### Summarize by Board Family\n```python\n# Merge with MES log to get board family\nmerged = pd.merge(defects_df, mes_log, on='run_id')\n\n# Group by board family\nby_family = merged.groupby('board_family')\n\n# Summary stats\nsummary = by_family.agg({\n    'fp_yield_pct': 'mean',\n    'boards_per_min': 'mean',\n    'count': 'sum'  # Total defects\n})\n```\n\n## Selection Criteria for Best Run\n\nRank runs per board family by:\n\n1. **First Pass Yield %** (primary criterion)\n   - Quality is paramount\n   - Higher yield = better process control\n   - Fewer rework costs\n\n2. **Boards Per Minute** (secondary criterion)\n   - Efficiency when quality is comparable\n   - Higher throughput = lower per-unit cost\n\n3. **Downtime** (tertiary criterion)\n   - Lower downtime = better reliability\n   - Indicates stable process\n\n4. **Critical Defect Count**\n   - Eliminate runs with critical defects immediately\n   - Even if yield% seems acceptable\n\n### Selection Algorithm\n```python\ndef select_best_run(board_family_runs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Select best run for a board family.\n\n    board_family_runs: DataFrame with all runs for one family\n\n    Returns: best_run_id (string)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter out runs with critical defects\n    clean_runs = board_family_runs[board_family_runs['critical_defects'] == 0]\n\n    if clean_runs.empty:\n        # If all have critical defects, choose lowest defect count\n        clean_runs = board_family_runs\n\n    # Sort by yield (descending), then BPM (descending)\n    ranked = clean_runs.sort_values(\n        by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n        ascending=[False, False]\n    )\n\n    return ranked.iloc[0]['run_id'], ranked.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n```\n\n## Comparison Framework\n\n### Run Comparison Metrics\n```python\ndef compare_runs(run_ids, merged_data):\n    \"\"\"Create comparison table for runs\"\"\"\n    comparison = merged_data[merged_data['run_id'].isin(run_ids)].groupby('run_id').agg({\n        'fp_yield_pct': 'first',\n        'boards_per_min': 'first',\n        'downtime_s': 'first',\n        'critical_defects': 'sum',\n        'total_defects': 'sum'\n    }).round(2)\n\n    return comparison.to_dict(orient='index')\n```\n\n## Statistical Validation\n\n### Check for Outliers\n```python\n# Remove obvious outliers (e.g., yield > 100%, BPM = 0)\nvalid_runs = merged_data[\n    (merged_data['fp_yield_pct'] <= 100) &\n    (merged_data['boards_per_min'] > 0) &\n    (merged_data['fp_yield_pct'] >= 0)\n]\n```\n\n### Normalize Metrics for Comparison\nWhen board families have very different specifications:\n\n```python\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import scaler.fit_transform(runs[['boards_per_min']])\n\n# Weighted composite score\ncomposite = 0.7 * yield_normalized + 0.3 * bpm_normalized\n```\n\n## Performance Trending\n\n### Track improvement over Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(max_ramp, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit'],\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'peak_temp_c': round(peak, 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': required_min\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'peak_temp_c': round(peak, 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': required_min\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! Maximum preheat ramp rate per run\n2. Time Above Liquidus (TAL) per run\n3. Peak temperature compliance\n4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n5. Best run selection per board family\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# ============================================================================\n# HANDBOOK SPECIFICATIONS (Extracted from handbook.pdf)\n# ============================================================================\n\nSPECIFICATIONS = {\n    'preheat_temp_min': 100,      # °C\n    'preheat_temp_max': 150,      # °C\n    'preheat_ramp_rate_limit': 2.0,  # °C/second - \"minimize rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\"\n\n    'tal_min': 30,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    'tal_max': 60,                # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(max_ramp, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit'],\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'peak_temp_c': round(peak, 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': required_min\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n\n    # Peak temperatures by solder type (from common industry standards)\n    # SAC alloys (lead-free)\n    'peak_temp_sac305': 217,      # liquidus temperature, peak >= 245°C typical\n    'peak_temp_sac0307': 217,\n    # Lead-based\n    'peak_temp_sn63pb37': 183,\n    'peak_temp_sn62pb36ag2': 179,\n\n    # Minimum peak temperature requirements (conservative)\n    'peak_temp_min_leadfree': 245,  # SAC alloys need at least 245°C peak\n    'peak_temp_min_leaded': 220,    # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(max_ramp, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit'],\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'peak_temp_c': round(peak, 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': required_min\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved!
