seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n\n    # Peak temperatures by solder type (from common industry standards)\n    # SAC alloys (lead-free)\n    'peak_temp_sac305': 217,      # liquidus temperature, peak >= 245°C typical\n    'peak_temp_sac0307': 217,\n    # Lead-based\n    'peak_temp_sn63pb37': 183,\n    'peak_temp_sn62pb36ag2': 179,\n\n    # Minimum peak temperature requirements (conservative)\n    'peak_temp_min_leadfree': 245,  # SAC alloys need at least 245°C peak\n    'peak_temp_min_leaded': 220,    # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': tc_id,\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(max_ramp, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit'],\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'peak_temp_c': round(float(peak), 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': int(required_min)\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': float(SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']),\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'peak_temp_c': round(float(peak), 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': int(required_min)\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': required_min_speed,\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = str(family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id'])\n            runner_up_ids = [str(rid) for rid in family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()]\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': str(board_family),\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nReflow Manufacturing Quality and Process Compliance Analysis\n\nSolves 5 questions about reflow manufacturing:\n1. Maximum preheat ramp rate per run\n2. Time Above Liquidus (TAL) per run\n3. Peak temperature compliance\n4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n5. Best run selection per board family\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# ============================================================================\n# HANDBOOK SPECIFICATIONS (Extracted from handbook.pdf)\n# ============================================================================\n\nSPECIFICATIONS = {\n    'preheat_temp_min': 100,      # °C\n    'preheat_temp_max': 150,      # °C\n    'preheat_ramp_rate_limit': 2.0,  # °C/second - \"minimize rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\"\n\n    'tal_min': 30,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    'tal_max': 60,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n\n    # Peak temperatures by solder type (from common industry standards)\n    # SAC alloys (lead-free)\n    'peak_temp_sac305': 217,      # liquidus temperature, peak >= 245°C typical\n    'peak_temp_sac0307': 217,\n    # Lead-based\n    'peak_temp_sn63pb37': 183,\n    'peak_temp_sn62pb36ag2': 179,\n\n    # Minimum peak temperature requirements (conservative)\n    'peak_temp_min_leadfree': 245,  # SAC alloys need at least 245°C peak\n    'peak_temp_min_leaded': 220,    # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': float(SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']),\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'peak_temp_c': round(float(peak), 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': int(required_min)\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = float(row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'])\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': float(required_min_speed),\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': bool(meets)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n            runner_up_ids = family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': board_family,\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! Maximum preheat ramp rate per run\n2. Time Above Liquidus (TAL) per run\n3. Peak temperature compliance\n4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n5. Best run selection per board family\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# ============================================================================\n# HANDBOOK SPECIFICATIONS (Extracted from handbook.pdf)\n# ============================================================================\n\nSPECIFICATIONS = {\n    'preheat_temp_min': 100,      # °C\n    'preheat_temp_max': 150,      # °C\n    'preheat_ramp_rate_limit': 2.0,  # °C/second - \"minimize rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\"\n\n    'tal_min': 30,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    'tal_max': 60,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n\n    # Peak temperatures by solder type (from common industry standards)\n    # SAC alloys (lead-free)\n    'peak_temp_sac305': 217,      # liquidus temperature, peak >= 245°C typical\n    'peak_temp_sac0307': 217,\n    # Lead-based\n    'peak_temp_sn63pb37': 183,\n    'peak_temp_sn62pb36ag2': 179,\n\n    # Minimum peak temperature requirements (conservative)\n    'peak_temp_min_leadfree': 245,  # SAC alloys need at least 245°C peak\n    'peak_temp_min_leaded': 220,    # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': float(SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']),\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'peak_temp_c': round(float(peak), 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': int(required_min)\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = float(row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'])\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': float(required_min_speed),\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': bool(meets)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = str(family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id'])\n            runner_up_ids = [str(rid) for rid in family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()]\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': str(board_family),\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved! Maximum preheat ramp rate per run\n2. Time Above Liquidus (TAL) per run\n3. Peak temperature compliance\n4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n5. Best run selection per board family\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nimport numpy as np\n\n\nclass NumpyEncoder(json.JSONEncoder):\n    \"\"\"Handle numpy data types in JSON encoding\"\"\"\n    def default(self, obj):\n        if isinstance(obj, np.integer):\n            return int(obj)\n        elif isinstance(obj, np.floating):\n            return float(obj)\n        elif isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n            return obj.tolist()\n        return super().default(obj)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# HANDBOOK SPECIFICATIONS (Extracted from handbook.pdf)\n# ============================================================================\n\nSPECIFICATIONS = {\n    'preheat_temp_min': 100,      # °C\n    'preheat_temp_max': 150,      # °C\n    'preheat_ramp_rate_limit': 2.0,  # °C/second - \"minimize rate of rise to less than 2°C/second\"\n\n    'tal_min': 30,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    'tal_max': 60,                # seconds - \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n\n    # Peak temperatures by solder type (from common industry standards)\n    # SAC alloys (lead-free)\n    'peak_temp_sac305': 217,      # liquidus temperature, peak >= 245°C typical\n    'peak_temp_sac0307': 217,\n    # Lead-based\n    'peak_temp_sn63pb37': 183,\n    'peak_temp_sn62pb36ag2': 179,\n\n    # Minimum peak temperature requirements (conservative)\n    'peak_temp_min_leadfree': 245,  # SAC alloys need at least 245°C peak\n    'peak_temp_min_leaded': 220,    # Leaded alloys need at least 220°C peak\n}\n\n# Solder alloy properties\nSOLDER_ALLOYS = {\n    'SAC305': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'SAC0307': {'liquidus': 217, 'type': 'leadfree'},\n    'Sn63Pb37': {'liquidus': 183, 'type': 'leaded'},\n    'Sn62Pb36Ag2': {'liquidus': 179, 'type': 'leaded'},\n}\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LOAD DATA\n# ============================================================================\n\nmes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\nthermocouple_data = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\ntest_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, tc_data, mes_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat for a given run.\n\n    Preheat: Temperature range 100-150°C\n    Ramp rate = dT/dt (°C/second)\n    Uses \"largest_mass\" thermocouple per handbook guidance.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run in preheat zone\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer \"largest_mass\" location (most thermally challenging)\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    # Pick first thermocouple if multiple exist\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    # Filter to preheat zone (100-150°C)\n    preheat = run_tc[\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] >= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_min']) &\n        (run_tc['temp_c'] <= SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_temp_max'])\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat) < 2:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            rate = (temps[i] - temps[i-1]) / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(rate)\n\n    if not ramp_rates:\n        return None, tc_id\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates)\n    return max_ramp, tc_id\n\n\ndef q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate():\n    \"\"\"Question 1: Calculate max preheat ramp rate per run\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n    violating_runs = []\n\n    # Get unique runs that have thermocouple data\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        max_ramp, tc_id = calculate_max_preheat_ramp(run_id, thermocouple_data, mes_log)\n\n        if max_ramp is not None:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s': round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violates limit\n            if max_ramp > SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']:\n                violating_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s': float(SPECIFICATIONS['preheat_ramp_rate_limit']),\n        'violating_runs': sorted(violating_runs),\n        'max_ramp_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_tal(run_id, tc_id, tc_data, liquidus_temp):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus for a specific run and thermocouple.\n\n    TAL = time from first point >= liquidus to last point >= liquidus\n    Handbook: \"30 to 60 seconds for most pastes\"\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['run_id'] == run_id) &\n        (tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None\n\n    # Filter to points at or above liquidus\n    above_liquidus = run_tc[run_tc['temp_c'] >= liquidus_temp]\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # TAL is the time span from first to last point above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n    tal = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    return tal\n\n\ndef q2_time_above_liquidus():\n    \"\"\"Question 2: Calculate TAL for each run\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique run, tc_id combinations in thermocouple data\n    run_tcs = thermocouple_data.groupby('run_id')['tc_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(run_tcs.index):\n        # Get liquidus temp from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus = float(run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0])\n\n        # Use largest_mass thermocouple if available\n        run_tc_data = thermocouple_data[thermocouple_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if 'largest_mass' in run_tc_data['tc_location'].values:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n        else:\n            preferred_tc = str(run_tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0])\n\n        tal = calculate_tal(run_id, preferred_tc, thermocouple_data, liquidus)\n\n        if tal is not None:\n            status = 'compliant' if (SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'] <= tal <= SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max']) else 'non-compliant'\n\n            results.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'tc_id': preferred_tc,\n                'tal_s': round(tal, 2),\n                'required_min_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_min'],\n                'required_max_tal_s': SPECIFICATIONS['tal_max'],\n                'status': status\n            })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirements\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef get_peak_temp_min(solder_alloy):\n    \"\"\"Get minimum required peak temperature based on solder type\"\"\"\n    # Determine if lead-free or leaded based on paste_type\n    if 'SAC' in solder_alloy:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leadfree']\n    else:\n        return SPECIFICATIONS['peak_temp_min_leaded']\n\n\ndef calculate_peak_temp(run_id, tc_data):\n    \"\"\"Get peak temperature for a run (use largest_mass TC)\"\"\"\n    run_tc = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if run_tc.empty:\n        return None, None\n\n    # Prefer largest_mass location\n    if 'largest_mass' in run_tc['tc_location'].values:\n        run_tc = run_tc[run_tc['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    tc_id = run_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n    peak = run_tc[run_tc['tc_id'] == tc_id]['temp_c'].max()\n\n    return peak, tc_id\n\n\ndef q3_peak_temperature_requirement():\n    \"\"\"Question 3: Check peak temperature compliance\"\"\"\n\n    failing_runs = []\n    results = {}\n\n    runs_with_tc = thermocouple_data['run_id'].unique()\n\n    for run_id in sorted(runs_with_tc):\n        # Get paste type from MES log\n        run_mes = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            # No MES data = failing\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n            continue\n\n        paste_type = run_mes['paste_type'].iloc[0]\n        required_min = get_peak_temp_min(paste_type)\n\n        peak, tc_id = calculate_peak_temp(run_id, thermocouple_data)\n\n        if peak is None:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing per requirements\n            failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        else:\n            results[run_id] = {\n                'tc_id': str(tc_id),\n                'peak_temp_c': round(float(peak), 2),\n                'required_min_peak_c': int(required_min)\n            }\n\n            if peak < required_min:\n                failing_runs.append(run_id)\n\n    output = {\n        'failing_runs': sorted(failing_runs),\n        'min_peak_by_run': results\n    }\n\n    return output\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_data, tal_value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate minimum required conveyor speed.\n\n    Based on thermal dwell requirements and board geometry.\n    For reflow ovens, conveyor speed must be slow enough to allow\n    sufficient thermal dwell in each zone.\n\n    Handbook mentions: Process speed should be determined by dwell time requirements.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_mes = mes_data[mes_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n    if run_mes.empty:\n        return None\n\n    board_length = run_mes['board_length_cm'].iloc[0]\n    thermal_mass_idx = run_mes['thermal_mass_idx'].iloc[0]\n\n    # Simplified dwell requirement model:\n    # Higher thermal mass needs longer dwell\n    # Estimate: minimum dwell ≈ 3-4 minutes for heavy boards, 2-3 for light\n    # Minimum speed in cm/min = board_length / (dwell_time_in_minutes)\n\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 0.55 (light): ~2 min dwell\n    # For thermal_mass_idx 1.25 (heavy): ~3.5 min dwell\n    dwell_minutes = 2 + (thermal_mass_idx - 0.55) * 2\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve this dwell\n    min_speed = board_length / dwell_minutes\n\n    return round(min_speed, 2)\n\n\ndef q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility():\n    \"\"\"Question 4: Check conveyor speed feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    for _, row in mes_log.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n        actual_speed = float(row['conveyor_speed_cm_min'])\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on thermal requirements\n        required_min_speed = calculate_required_min_speed(run_id, mes_log, None)\n\n        if required_min_speed is None:\n            continue\n\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'required_min_speed_cm_min': float(required_min_speed),\n            'actual_speed_cm_min': round(actual_speed, 2),\n            'meets': bool(meets)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef q5_best_run_selection():\n    \"\"\"Question 5: Select best run per board family\"\"\"\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get FPY (First Pass Yield) from test defects - use SUMMARY rows only\n    fpy_data = test_defects[test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    fpy_data = fpy_data[['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']].drop_duplicates(subset=['run_id'])\n\n    # Merge with MES log\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        mes_log[['run_id', 'board_family', 'boards_per_min', 'downtime_s']],\n        fpy_data,\n        on='run_id',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    # For runs without FPY data, estimate based on presence in test data\n    merged['has_test_data'] = merged['run_id'].isin(test_defects['run_id'].unique())\n\n    # Fill missing FPY with defaults\n    merged['fp_yield_pct'] = merged['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Group by board family and rank\n    for board_family in sorted(merged['board_family'].unique()):\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Sort by: FPY (desc), then BPM (desc)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'boards_per_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        if len(family_runs) > 0:\n            best_run = str(family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id'])\n            runner_up_ids = [str(rid) for rid in family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist()]\n\n            results.append({\n                'board_family': str(board_family),\n                'best_run_id': best_run,\n                'runner_up_run_ids': sorted(runner_up_ids)\n            })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN EXECUTION\n# ============================================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    print(\"Solving Question 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate...\")\n    q1_result = q1_max_preheat_ramp_rate()\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q1: {len(q1_result['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q1_result['violating_runs'])} violating\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 2: Time Above Liquidus...\")\n    q2_result = q2_time_above_liquidus()\n    with open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q2_result, f, indent=2)\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in q2_result if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"✓ Q2: {len(q2_result)} runs analyzed, {compliant} compliant\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 3: Peak Temperature...\")\n    q3_result = q3_peak_temperature_requirement()\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q3: {len(q3_result['min_peak_by_run'])} runs analyzed, {len(q3_result['failing_runs'])} failing\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 4: Conveyor Speed...\")\n    q4_result = q4_conveyor_speed_feasibility()\n    with open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q4_result, f, indent=2)\n    feasible = sum(1 for r in q4_result if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"✓ Q4: {len(q4_result)} runs analyzed, {feasible} feasible\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving Question 5: Best Run Selection...\")\n    q5_result = q5_best_run_selection()\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_result, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"✓ Q5: {len(q5_result)} board families analyzed\")\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ All questions solved!
